mean over time and trials\n    \n    # Filter low FR units\n    fr_mask = mean_frs > LOW_FR\n    \n    if np.sum(fr_mask) == 0:\n        return None, []\n    \n    firing_rates = firing_rates[fr_mask]\n    kept_units = [valid_units[i] for i in range(len(valid_units)) if fr_mask[i]]\n    \n    return firing_rates, kept_units\n\n\ndef extract_feature_velocity(session, cam_idx, feat_name, time_axis, vidshift):\n    \"\"\"Extract velocity for a DLC feature, matching findPosition + findVelocity.\n    Returns: velocity magnitude (n_timepoints, n_trials)\n    \"\"\"\n    Ntrials = session['bp']['Ntrials']\n    goCue = session['bp']['ev']['goCue']\n    \n    cam_data = session['traj'][cam_idx]\n    feat_names = cam_data['feat_names']\n    \n    # Find feature index\n    feat_idx = None\n    for i, name in enumerate(feat_names):\n        if name.lower() == feat_name.lower():\n            feat_idx = i\n            break\n    \n    if feat_idx is None:\n        return np.full((len(time_axis), Ntrials), np.nan)\n    \n    is_tongue = 'tongue' in feat_name.lower()\n    \n    xpos = np.full((len(time_axis), Ntrials), np.nan)\n    ypos = np.full((len(time_axis), Ntrials), np.nan)\n    \n    for trix in range(Ntrials):\n        trial = cam_data['trials'][trix]\n        \n        if trial['ts'] is None:\n            continue\n        if np.isnan(trial['NdroppedFrames']):\n            continue\n        \n        ts = trial['ts']  # (n_feats, 3, n_timepoints) in h5py\n        \n        # Get frame times\n        if trial['frameTimes'] is not None and not np.all(np.isnan(trial['frameTimes'])):\n            frame_times = trial['frameTimes']\n        else:\n            n_frames = ts.shape[2]\n            frame_times = np.arange(1, n_frames + 1) / VIDEO_FS\n        \n        # Get x, y for this feature\n        x = ts[feat_idx, 0, :]  # x coordinate\n        y = ts[feat_idx, 1, :]  # y coordinate\n        \n        # Align time to goCue\n        aligned_times = frame_times - vidshift - goCue[trix]\n        \n        # Smooth non-tongue features (mySmooth with N=1 and 'reflect')\n        # In the reference code, non-tongue features are smoothed with mySmooth(ts, 1, 'reflect')\n        # N=1 means no smoothing\n        \n        # Interpolate to time axis\n        try:\n            valid = ~np.isnan(x) & ~np.isnan(aligned_times)\n            if np.sum(valid) < 2:\n                continue\n            \n            f_x = interp1d(aligned_times[valid], x[valid], kind='linear',\n                          bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n            f_y = interp1d(aligned_times[valid], y[valid], kind='linear',\n                          bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n            \n            xpos[:, trix] = f_x(time_axis)\n            ypos[:, trix] = f_y(time_axis)\n        except:\n            continue\n        \n        # Fill missing for non-tongue\n        if not is_tongue:\n            mask_nan = np.isnan(xpos[:, trix])\n            if np.any(mask_nan) and not np.all(mask_nan):\n                xpos[:, trix] = _fill_nearest(xpos[:, trix])\n                ypos[:, trix] = _fill_nearest(ypos[:, trix])\n    \n    # Compute velocity (gradient, matching findVelocity.m)\n    xvel = np.full_like(xpos, np.nan)\n    yvel = np.full_like(ypos, np.nan)\n    \n    for trix in range(Ntrials):\n        if np.all(np.isnan(xpos[:, trix])):\n            continue\n        \n        xv = np.gradient(xpos[:, trix])\n        yv = np.gradient(ypos[:, trix])\n        \n        if not is_tongue:\n            # Subtract baseline derivative\n            base_x = np.nanmedian(np.diff(xpos[:, trix]))\n            base_y = np.nanmedian(np.diff(ypos[:, trix]))\n            xv = xv - base_x\n            yv = yv - base_y\n            # Fill missing\n            xv = _fill_nearest(xv)\n            yv = _fill_nearest(yv)\n        else:\n            # Set tongue velocity to 0 if not visible\n            xv[np.isnan(xv)] = 0\n            yv[np.isnan(yv)] = 0\n        \n        xvel[:, trix] = xv\n        yvel[:, trix] = yv\n    \n    # Compute velocity magnitude\n    vel_mag = np.sqrt(xvel**2 + yvel**2)\n    \n    return vel_mag\n\n\ndef _fill_nearest(arr):\n    \"\"\"Fill NaN values with nearest non-NaN value.\"\"\"\n    mask = np.isnan(arr)\n    if not np.any(mask) or np.all(mask):\n        return arr\n    arr = arr.copy()\n    # Forward fill then backward fill\n    valid = np.where(~mask)[0]\n    if len(valid) == 0:\n        return arr\n    for i in range(len(arr)):\n        if mask[i]:\n            # Find nearest valid\n            dists = np.abs(valid - i)\n            arr[i] = arr[valid[np.argmin(dists)]]\n    return arr\n\n\ndef align_motion_energy(me_data, session, time_axis, vidshift):\n    \"\"\"Align motion energy to goCue, matching loadMotionEnergy.m.\"\"\"\n    Ntrials = session['bp']['Ntrials']\n    goCue = session['bp']['ev']['goCue']\n    \n    me_aligned = np.full((len(time_axis), Ntrials), np.nan)\n    \n    n_me_trials = len(me_data['data'])\n    \n    for trix in range(min(Ntrials, n_me_trials)):\n        trial_me = me_data['data'][trix]\n        if trial_me.size == 0:\n            continue\n        \n        # Get frame times for this trial\n        cam_data = session['traj'][0]  # camera 0\n        trial_traj = cam_data['trials'][trix] if trix < len(cam_data['trials']) else None\n        \n        if trial_traj is not None and trial_traj['frameTimes'] is not None and not np.all(np.isnan(trial_traj['frameTimes'])):\n            frame_times = trial_traj['frameTimes']\n            aligned_times = frame_times - vidshift - goCue[trix]\n        else:\n            # Fallback: create frame times\n            n_frames = trial_me.shape[0]\n            if trial_traj is not None and trial_traj['ts'] is not None:\n                n_frames = trial_traj['ts'].shape[2]\n            frame_times = np.arange(1, n_frames + 1) / VIDEO_FS\n            aligned_times = frame_times - 0.5 - goCue[trix]\n        \n        # Trim to same length\n        n = min(len(trial_me), len(aligned_times))\n        if n < 2:\n            continue\n        \n        try:\n            f_me = interp1d(aligned_times[:n], trial_me[:n], kind='linear',\n                           bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n            me_aligned[:, trix] = f_me(time_axis)\n        except:\n            continue\n    \n    # Fill NaN with nearest\n    for trix in range(Ntrials):\n        if not np.all(np.isnan(me_aligned[:, trix])):\n            me_aligned[:, trix] = _fill_nearest(me_aligned[:, trix])\n    \n    return me_aligned\n\n\ndef get_available_sessions():\n    \"\"\"Get list of all available sessions with their metadata.\"\"\"\n    sessions = []\n    \n    # Parse loading scripts to get probe info\n    script_dir = 'code/DataLoadingScripts/Recording and video'\n    probe_map = {}  # (animal, date) -> probes\n    \n    for f in sorted(os.listdir(script_dir)):\n        if f.endswith('.m'):\n            with open(os.path.join(script_dir, f)) as fh:\n                content = fh.read()\n            anm = None\n            date = None\n            for line in content.split('\\n'):\n                line = line.strip()\n                if line.startswith('%'):\n                    continue\n                m = re.search(r\"meta\\(end(?:\\+1)?\\)\\.anm\\s*=\\s*'(\\w+)'\", line)\n                if m:\n                    anm = m.group(1)\n                m = re.search(r\"meta\\(end(?:\\+1)?\\)\\.date\\s*=\\s*'([\\d-]+)'\", line)\n                if m:\n                    date = m.group(1)\n                m = re.search(r\"meta\\(end(?:\\+1)?\\)\\.probe\\s*=\\s*(.+?);\", line)\n                if m:\n                    probe_str = m.group(1).strip()\n                    if '[' in probe_str:\n                        probes = [int(x) for x in re.findall(r'\\d+', probe_str)]\n                    else:\n                        probes = [int(probe_str)]\n                    if anm and date:\n                        probe_map[(anm, date)] = probes\n    \n    # Find all data files\n    for data_dir in ['data/Ephys_Behavior', 'data/RandomizedDelay_Ephys_Behavior']:\n        if not os.path.exists(data_dir):\n            continue\n        for fn in sorted(os.listdir(data_dir)):\n            if not fn.startswith('data_structure_'):\n                continue\n            m = re.match(r'data_structure_(\\w+)_(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2})\\.mat', fn)\n            if not m:\n                continue\n            \n            animal = m.group(1)\n            date = m.group(2)\n            \n            # Skip behavior-only sessions (MAH animals)\n            if animal.startswith('MAH'):\n                continue\n            \n            filepath = os.path.join(data_dir, fn)\n            \n            # Get probe info from loading scripts\n            probes = probe_map.get((animal, date), [1])  # default to probe 1\n            \n            # Check for motion energy file\n            me_fn = fn.replace('data_structure_', 'motionEnergy_')\n            me_filepath = os.path.join(data_dir, me_fn)\n            has_me = os.path.exists(me_filepath)\n            \n            sessions.append({\n                'animal': animal,\n                'date': date,\n                'filepath': filepath,\n                'probes': probes,\n                'me_filepath': me_filepath if has_me else None,\n                'data_dir': data_dir\n            })\n    \n    return sessions\n\n\ndef check_session_inclusion(session_data, probes):\n    \"\"\"Check if session meets inclusion criteria.\n    Matching UseInclusionCritera.m: >40 R hit DR trials AND >40 L hit DR trials.\n    \"\"\"\n    bp = session_data['bp']\n    \n    # Count R hit DR trials (no stim, no autowater, no early)\n    r_hit_dr = bp['R'] & bp['hit'] & ~bp['stim_enable'] & ~bp['autowater'] & ~bp['early']\n    l_hit_dr = bp['L'] & bp['hit'] & ~bp['stim_enable'] & ~bp['autowater'] & ~bp['early']\n    \n    n_r = np.sum(r_hit_dr)\n    n_l = np.sum(l_hit_dr)\n    \n    return n_r > MIN_HIT_TRIALS and n_l > MIN_HIT_TRIALS, n_r, n_l\n\n\ndef process_session(sess_info, time_axis, show_processing=False):\n    \"\"\"Process a single session and return formatted data.\"\"\"\n    animal = sess_info['animal']\n    date = sess_info['date']\n    probes = sess_info['probes']\n    \n    print(f'  Loading {animal}_{date}...')\n    t0 = time.time()\n    \n    session = load_session_data(sess_info['filepath'])\n    t_load = time.time() - t0\n    print(f'    Loaded in {t_load:.1f}s')\n    \n    # Check inclusion criteria\n    included, n_r, n_l = check_session_inclusion(session, probes)\n    if not included:\n        print(f'    EXCLUDED: R hit DR={n_r}, L hit DR={n_l} (need >{MIN_HIT_TRIALS} each)')\n        return None\n    print(f'    R hit DR={n_r}, L hit DR={n_l}')\n    \n    # Process spikes for each probe and combine\n    t0 = time.time()\n    all_firing_rates = []\n    for p in probes:\n        p_idx = p - 1  # Convert to 0-indexed\n        if p_idx >= len(session['clu']):\n            print(f'    Warning: probe {p} not found, skipping')\n            continue\n        fr, kept = process_spikes(session, p_idx, time_axis)\n        if fr is not None and len(kept) > 0:\n            all_firing_rates.append(fr)\n            print(f'    Probe {p}: {fr.shape[0]} units kept')\n    \n    if len(all_firing_rates) == 0:\n        print(f'    EXCLUDED: no valid units')\n        return None\n    \n    # Concatenate across probes\n    firing_rates = np.concatenate(all_firing_rates, axis=0)  # (n_neurons, n_time, n_trials)\n    n_neurons = firing_rates.shape[0]\n    t_spk = time.time() - t0\n    print(f'    Spike processing: {t_spk:.1f}s, {n_neurons} total units')\n    \n    if n_neurons < MIN_UNITS:\n        print(f'    EXCLUDED: only {n_neurons} units (need >={MIN_UNITS})')\n        return None\n    \n    # ---- Compute video offset ----\n    vidshift = compute_video_offset(session)\n    \n    # ---- Extract tongue velocity ----\n    t0 = time.time()\n    tongue_vel = extract_feature_velocity(session, 0, 'tongue', time_axis, vidshift)\n    t_tongue = time.time() - t0\n    print(f'    Tongue velocity: {t_tongue:.1f}s')\n    \n    # ---- Extract paw velocity (top_paw from camera 1) ----\n    t0 = time.time()\n    paw_vel = extract_feature_velocity(session, 1, 'top_paw', time_axis, vidshift)\n    t_paw = time.time() - t0\n    print(f'    Paw velocity: {t_paw:.1f}s')\n    \n    # ---- Load and align motion energy ----\n    t0 = time.time()\n    if sess_info['me_filepath'] is not None:\n        me_data = load_motion_energy(sess_info['me_filepath'])\n        if me_data is not None:\n            me_aligned = align_motion_energy(me_data, session, time_axis, vidshift)\n        else:\n            me_aligned = np.full((len(time_axis), session['bp']['Ntrials']), np.nan)\n    else:\n        me_aligned = np.full((len(time_axis), session['bp']['Ntrials']), np.nan)\n    t_me = time.time() - t0\n    print(f'    Motion energy: {t_me:.1f}s')\n    \n    # ---- Select valid trials ----\n    # Include all trials that are not early and not stim\n    bp = session['bp']\n    valid_trials = ~bp['early'] & ~bp['stim_enable']\n    # Also exclude 'no' (ignore) trials\n    valid_trials = valid_trials & ~bp['no']\n    \n    trial_indices = np.where(valid_trials)[0]\n    \n    if len(trial_indices) < 2:\n        print(f'    EXCLUDED: only {len(trial_indices)} valid trials')\n        return None\n    \n    print(f'    Valid trials: {len(trial_indices)} / {bp[\"Ntrials\"]}')\n    \n    # ---- Compute per-session thresholds for discretization ----\n    # Tongue velocity threshold: 50th percentile of all valid trial data\n    tongue_valid = tongue_vel[:, trial_indices]\n    tongue_thresh = np.nanpercentile(tongue_valid[~np.isnan(tongue_valid)], 50) if np.any(~np.isnan(tongue_valid)) else 0\n    \n    paw_valid = paw_vel[:, trial_indices]\n    paw_thresh = np.nanpercentile(paw_valid[~np.isnan(paw_valid)], 50) if np.any(~np.isnan(paw_valid)) else 0\n    \n    me_valid = me_aligned[:, trial_indices]\n    me_thresh = np.nanpercentile(me_valid[~np.isnan(me_valid)], 50) if np.any(~np.isnan(me_valid)) else 0\n    \n    print(f'    Thresholds: tongue={tongue_thresh:.2f}, paw={paw_thresh:.2f}, ME={me_thresh:.4f}')\n    \n    # ---- Build trial-level data ----\n    neural_trials = []\n    input_trials = []\n    output_trials = []\n    \n    for trial_idx in trial_indices:\n        # Neural: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n        neural = firing_rates[:, :, trial_idx].astype(np.float32)\n        neural_trials.append(neural)\n        \n        # Input: time from goCue (same for all trials)\n        inp = time_axis.astype(np.float32).reshape(1, -1)  # (1, n_timepoints)\n        input_trials.append(inp)\n        \n        # Output\n        # Lick direction: R=1, L=0\n        lick_dir = 1 if bp['R'][trial_idx] else 0\n        \n        # Behavioral context: WC=0, DR=1\n        context = 0 if bp['autowater'][trial_idx] else 1\n        \n        # Outcome: correct=1, incorrect=0\n        outcome = 1 if bp['hit'][trial_idx] else 0\n        \n        # Tongue velocity discretized (time-varying)\n        tv = tongue_vel[:, trial_idx].copy()\n        tv_disc = np.zeros(len(time_axis), dtype=np.int64)\n        tv_disc[tv >= tongue_thresh] = 1\n        # Handle NaN: set to 0 (below threshold)\n        tv_disc[np.isnan(tv)] = 0\n        \n        # Paw velocity discretized (time-varying)\n        pv = paw_vel[:, trial_idx].copy()\n        pv_disc = np.zeros(len(time_axis), dtype=np.int64)\n        pv_disc[pv >= paw_thresh] = 1\n        pv_disc[np.isnan(pv)] = 0\n        \n        # Motion energy discretized (time-varying)\n        me = me_aligned[:, trial_idx].copy()\n        me_disc = np.zeros(len(time_axis), dtype=np.int64)\n        me_disc[me >= me_thresh] = 1\n        me_disc[np.isnan(me)] = 0\n        \n        # Stack outputs: (n_output, n_timepoints) for time-varying,\n        # or (n_output,) for per-trial\n        # Per-trial: lick_dir, context, outcome\n        # Time-varying: tongue_vel, paw_vel, motion_energy\n        out = np.zeros((6, len(time_axis)), dtype=np.int64)\n        out[0, :] = lick_dir  # constant across time\n        out[1, :] = context   # constant across time\n        out[2, :] = outcome   # constant across time\n        out[3, :] = tv_disc\n        out[4, :] = pv_disc\n        out[5, :] = me_disc\n        \n        output_trials.append(out)\n    \n    # ---- Visualization ----\n    if show_processing:\n        plot_processing(animal, date, session, firing_rates, tongue_vel, paw_vel,\n                       me_aligned, time_axis, trial_indices, tongue_thresh, paw_thresh, me_thresh)\n    \n    result = {\n        'neural': neural_trials,\n        'input': input_trials,\n        'output': output_trials,\n        'animal': animal,\n        'date': date,\n        'n_neurons': n_neurons,\n        'n_trials': len(trial_indices),\n    }\n    \n    return result\n\n\ndef plot_processing(animal, date, session, firing_rates, tongue_vel, paw_vel,\n                   me_aligned, time_axis, trial_indices, tongue_thresh, paw_thresh, me_thresh):\n    \"\"\"Plot processing steps for visual verification.\"\"\"\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n    \n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(4, 3, figsize=(20, 16))\n    fig.suptitle(f'{animal}_{date}', fontsize=14)\n    \n    bp = session['bp']\n    \n    # Row 1: Neural activity examples\n    n_neurons = firing_rates.shape[0]\n    example_neurons = np.random.choice(n_neurons, min(3, n_neurons), replace=False)\n    for col, neuron_idx in enumerate(example_neurons):\n        ax = axes[0, col]\n        # Plot a few trials\n        n_show = min(10, len(trial_indices))\n        for t in range(n_show):\n            trix = trial_indices[t]\n            ax.plot(time_axis, firing_rates[neuron_idx, :, trix], alpha=0.3)\n        ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5, label='Go cue')\n        ax.set_title(f'Neuron {neuron_idx}')\n        ax.set_xlabel('Time from goCue (s)')\n        ax.set_ylabel('Firing rate (Hz)')\n    \n    # Row 2: Tongue velocity\n    ax = axes[1, 0]\n    for t in range(min(10, len(trial_indices))):\n        trix = trial_indices[t]\n        ax.plot(time_axis, tongue_vel[:, trix], alpha=0.3)\n    ax.axhline(tongue_thresh, color='r', linestyle='--', label=f'50th pct={tongue_thresh:.1f}')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n    ax.set_title('Tongue velocity')\n    ax.legend()\n    \n    # Row 2: Paw velocity\n    ax = axes[1, 1]\n    for t in range(min(10, len(trial_indices))):\n        trix = trial_indices[t]\n        ax.plot(time_axis, paw_vel[:, trix], alpha=0.3)\n    ax.axhline(paw_thresh, color='r', linestyle='--', label=f'50th pct={paw_thresh:.1f}')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n    ax.set_title('Paw velocity')\n    ax.legend()\n    \n    # Row 2: Motion energy\n    ax = axes[1, 2]\n    for t in range(min(10, len(trial_indices))):\n        trix = trial_indices[t]\n        ax.plot(time_axis, me_aligned[:, trix], alpha=0.3)\n    ax.axhline(me_thresh, color='r', linestyle='--', label=f'50th pct={me_thresh:.4f}')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n    ax.set_title('Motion energy')\n    ax.legend()\n    \n    # Row 3: Discretized outputs example trial\n    ex_trix = trial_indices[0]\n    ax = axes[2, 0]\n    tv_disc = (tongue_vel[:, ex_trix] >= tongue_thresh).astype(int)\n    ax.plot(time_axis, tv_disc, 'b-', label='Tongue vel disc')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n    ax.set_title(f'Discretized tongue vel (trial {ex_trix})')\n    ax.set_ylim(-0.1, 1.1)\n    \n    ax = axes[2, 1]\n    pv_disc = (paw_vel[:, ex_trix] >= paw_thresh).astype(int)\n    ax.plot(time_axis, pv_disc, 'g-', label='Paw vel disc')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n    ax.set_title(f'Discretized paw vel (trial {ex_trix})')\n    ax.set_ylim(-0.1, 1.1)\n    \n    ax = axes[2, 2]\n    me_disc = (me_aligned[:, ex_trix] >= me_thresh).astype(int)\n    ax.plot(time_axis, me_disc, 'r-', label='ME disc')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n    ax.set_title(f'Discretized ME (trial {ex_trix})')\n    ax.set_ylim(-0.1, 1.1)\n    \n    # Row 4: Trial outcome distributions\n    ax = axes[3, 0]\n    valid = trial_indices\n    r_count = np.sum(bp['R'][valid])\n    l_count = np.sum(bp['L'][valid])\n    ax.bar(['Right', 'Left'], [r_count, l_count])\n    ax.set_title('Lick direction')\n    \n    ax = axes[3, 1]\n    dr_count = np.sum(~bp['autowater'][valid])\n    wc_count = np.sum(bp['autowater'][valid])\n    ax.bar(['DR', 'WC'], [dr_count, wc_count])\n    ax.set_title('Context')\n    \n    ax = axes[3, 2]\n    hit_count = np.sum(bp['hit'][valid])\n    miss_count = np.sum(bp['miss'][valid])\n    ax.bar(['Hit', 'Miss'], [hit_count, miss_count])\n    ax.set_title('Outcome')\n    \n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(f'processing_{animal}_{date}.png', dpi=100)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'    Saved processing_{animal}_{date}.png')\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert Hasnain et al. 2024 data')\n    parser.add_argument('output', help='Output pickle file path')\n    parser.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='Process only 2 sessions')\n    parser.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='Process all sessions (default)')\n    parser.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true', help='Plot processing visualizations')\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    \n    total_start = time.time()\n    \n    # Get available sessions\n    all_sessions = get_available_sessions()\n    print(f'Found {len(all_sessions)} total sessions')\n    \n    if args.sample:\n        # Select 2 sessions for testing\n        all_sessions = all_sessions[:2]\n        print(f'Sample mode: processing {len(all_sessions)} sessions')\n    \n    # gaussian kernel with window size N\r\n% varargin:\r\n%   'reflect' - reflect N elements from beginning of time series across\r\n%               0 index\r\n%   'zeropad' - pad N zeros across 0 index\r\n%   'none'    - don't handle boundary conditions\r\n\r\n% returns:\r\n% - out: filtered data, same size as x\r\n% - kernsd: std dev of gaussian kernel\r\n\r\n% % example usage:\r\n% x = 5*sin(1:0.01:100)';\r\n% y = x + randn(size(x));\r\n% N = 51;\r\n% out = mySmooth(y,N,'reflect');\r\n%\r\n% figure;\r\n% subplot(3,1,1); plot(x);\r\n% xlim([-100 200])\r\n% subplot(3,1,2); plot(y)\r\n% xlim([-100 200])\r\n% subplot(3,1,3); plot(out); %hold on; plot(out,'.','MarkerSize',6)\r\n% xlim([-100 200])\r\n\r\n\r\n%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%\r\n\r\nif N == 0 || N == 1 % no smoothing\r\n    out = x;\r\n    kernsd = 0;\r\n    return\r\nend\r\n\r\nif isrow(x) % if 1-d time series, ensure column vector\r\n    x = x';\r\nend\r\n\r\nif nargin > 2\r\n    % handle a boundary condition\r\n    bctype = varargin{1};\r\n    if strcmpi(bctype,'reflect')\r\n        x_filt = cat(1,x(1:N,:),x);\r\n        trim = N + 1;\r\n    elseif strcmpi(bctype,'zeropad')\r\n        x_filt = cat(1,zeros(N,size(x,2)),x);\r\n        trim = N + 1;\r\n    elseif strcmpi(bctype,'none')\r\n        x_filt = x;\r\n        trim = 1;\r\n    else\r\n        warning('invalid boundary condition type - regular smoothing');\r\n        x_filt = x;\r\n        trim = 1;\r\n    end\r\nelse\r\n    % no bc handling\r\n    x_filt = x;\r\n    trim = 1;\r\nend\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\nNcol = size(x_filt, 2);\r\nNel = size(x_filt, 1);\r\n\r\nkern = gausswin(N);\r\nkernsd = std(1:N);\r\n\r\n\r\nkern(1:floor(numel(kern)/2)) = 0; %causal\r\nkern = kern./sum(kern);\r\n\r\nout = zeros(Nel, Ncol);\r\nfor j = 1:Ncol\r\n    out(:, j) = conv(x_filt(:, j), kern, 'same');\r\nend\r\nout = out(trim:end,:);\r\n\r\nendroot@817026890af1:/app#\r\n\n\n=== Step 49 ===\nNew Terminal Output:\n:);\r\n\r\nendroot@817026890af1:/app# code/funcs/kinematics/findVelocity.m\r\nfunction [xvel, yvel] = findVelocity(xpos, ypos, feat)\r\n\r\n\r\nfor i = 1:size(xpos,2) % for each trial\r\n    tsinterp = [xpos(:,i) ypos(:,i)];\r\n gaussian kernel with window size N\r\n% varargin:\r\n%   'reflect' - reflect N elements from beginning of time series across\r\n%               0 index\r\n%   'zeropad' - pad N zeros across 0 index\r\n%   'none'    - don't handle boundary conditions\r\n\r\n% returns:\r\n% - out: filtered data, same size as x\r\n% - kernsd: std dev of gaussian kernel\r\n\r\n% % example usage:\r\n% x = 5*sin(1:0.01:100)';\r\n% y = x + randn(size(x));\r\n% N = 51;\r\n% out = mySmooth(y,N,'reflect');\r\n%\r\n% figure;\r\n% subplot(3,1,1); plot(x);\r\n% xlim([-100 200])\r\n% subplot(3,1,2); plot(y)\r\n% xlim([-100 200])\r\n% subplot(3,1,3); plot(out); %hold on; plot(out,'.','MarkerSize',6)\r\n% xlim([-100 200])\r\n\r\n\r\n%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%\r\n\r\nif N == 0 || N == 1 % no smoothing\r\n    out = x;\r\n    kernsd = 0;\r\n    return\r\nend\r\n\r\nif isrow(x) % if 1-d time series, ensure column vector\r\n    x = x';\r\nend\r\n\r\nif nargin > 2\r\n    % handle a boundary condition\r\n    bctype = varargin{1};\r\n    if strcmpi(bctype,'reflect')\r\n        x_filt = cat(1,x(1:N,:),x);\r\n        trim = N + 1;\r\n    elseif strcmpi(bctype,'zeropad')\r\n        x_filt = cat(1,zeros(N,size(x,2)),x);\r\n        trim = N + 1;\r\n    elseif strcmpi(bctype,'none')\r\n        x_filt = x;\r\n        trim = 1;\r\n    else\r\n        warning('invalid boundary condition type - regular smoothing');\r\n        x_filt = x;\r\n        trim = 1;\r\n    end\r\nelse\r\n    % no bc handling\r\n    x_filt = x;\r\n    trim = 1;\r\nend\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\nNcol = size(x_filt, 2);\r\nNel = size(x_filt, 1);\r\n\r\nkern = gausswin(N);\r\nkernsd = std(1:N);\r\n\r\n\r\nkern(1:floor(numel(kern)/2)) = 0; %causal\r\nkern = kern./sum(kern);\r\n\r\nout = zeros(Nel, Ncol);\r\nfor j = 1:Ncol\r\n    out(:, j) = conv(x_filt(:, j), kern, 'same');\r\nend\r\nout = out(trim:end,:);\r\n\r\nendroot@817026890af1:/app#\r\n\n\n=== Step 49 ===\nNew Terminal Output:\n:);\r\n\r\nendroot@817026890af1:/app# The AI uses `hit` to determine correct trials, with all non-hit trials coded as incorrect.\n\n## 6-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Outcome*?\n\ni. Matches the instruction specification: \"incorrect = 0, correct = 1, per-trial\".\n\n## 7-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Tongue velocity* derived from?\n\ni. The AI identified from `getKinematicsFromVideo.m` that tongue is from camera 0 (side camera), matching `params.traj_features{1}` which includes 'tongue'.\n\n## 7-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Tongue velocity*?\n\ni. Processing: (1) Extract x, y positions from DLC tracking for 'tongue' feature, (2) Compute video offset using `findVideoOffset`, (3) Align frame times to goCue: `frameTimes - vidshift - goCue[trial]`, (4) Interpolate positions to neural time axis, (5) Compute velocity via `np.gradient()` on x and y, (6) Set NaN velocities to 0 (tongue not visible), (7) Compute velocity magnitude: `sqrt(xvel^2 + yvel^2)`.\n\nii.\n```python\n# Align and interpolate\naligned_times = frame_times - vidshift - goCue[trix]\nf_x = interp1d(aligned_times[valid], x[valid], kind='linear', ...)\nxpos[:, trix] = f_x(time_axis)\n# Velocity\nxv = np.gradient(xpos[:, trix])\n# Tongue-specific: NaN -> 0\nxv[np.isnan(xv)] = 0\nyv[np.isnan(yv)] = 0\n# Magnitude\nvel_mag = np.sqrt(xvel**2 + yvel**2)\n```\n\niii. Matches the reference code in `findPosition.m` (interpolation) and `findVelocity.m` (gradient, NaN->0 for tongue). The AI noted that tongue features are NOT smoothed before interpolation and NaN velocity is set to 0 (tongue not visible).\n\n## 7-c. How is `output` *Tongue velocity* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Per-session 50th percentile threshold computed from all valid trial data. Values >= threshold are coded as 1 (high), values < threshold as 0 (low). NaN values are set to 0.\n\nii.\n```python\ntongue_valid = tongue_vel[:, trial_indices]\ntongue_thresh = np.nanpercentile(tongue_valid[~np.isnan(tongue_valid)], 50)\n# ...\ntv_disc = np.zeros(len(time_axis), dtype=np.int64)\ntv_disc[tv >= tongue_thresh] = 1\ntv_disc[np.isnan(tv)] = 0\n```\n\niii. Follows instruction: \"discretized into two bins with per-session threshold: 0: < 50th percentile, 1: >= 50th percentile\". However, the tongue velocity distribution is heavily skewed because NaN tongue values (tongue not visible) are set to 0 velocity, making the 50th percentile very small or zero. This causes extreme class imbalance (5.7% low, 94.3% high across the dataset).\n\n## 7-d. How is `output` *Tongue velocity* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. Frame times are aligned to goCue with video offset correction, then positions are interpolated to the neural time axis using linear interpolation. Velocity is computed on the interpolated positions.\n\nii.\n```python\naligned_times = frame_times - vidshift - goCue[trix]\nf_x = interp1d(aligned_times[valid], x[valid], kind='linear', ...)\nxpos[:, trix] = f_x(time_axis)\n```\n\niii. Note: since `no` (ignore) trials are filtered out, the remaining non-hit trials are `miss` trials.\n\nii.\n```python\noutcome = 1 if bp['hit'][trial_idx] else 0\nout[2, :] = outcome\n```\n\niii. Matches the instruction specification: \"incorrect = 0, correct = 1, per-trial\".\n\n## 7-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Tongue velocity* derived from?\n\ni. Derived from `obj.traj{1}` (camera 0 / side camera) trajectory data. Specifically, the 'tongue' feature's x and y coordinates from the DLC tracking: `traj.ts[feat_idx, 0:2, :]` (x, y positions over time) and `traj.frameTimes` for temporal alignment.\n\nii.\n```python\ntongue_vel = extract_feature_velocity(session, 0, 'tongue', time_axis, vidshift)\n# In extract_feature_velocity:\nx = ts[feat_idx, 0, :]  # x coordinate\ny = ts[feat_idx, 1, :]  # y coordinate\n```\n\niii. The AI identified from `getKinematicsFromVideo.m` that tongue is from camera 0 (side camera), matching `params.traj_features{1}` which includes 'tongue'.\n\n## 7-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Tongue velocity*?\n\ni. Processing: (1) Extract x, y positions from DLC tracking for 'tongue' feature, (2) Compute video offset using `findVideoOffset`, (3) Align frame times to goCue: `frameTimes - vidshift - goCue[trial]`, (4) Interpolate positions to neural time axis, (5) Compute velocity via `np.gradient()` on x and y, (6) Set NaN velocities to 0 (tongue not visible), (7) Compute velocity magnitude: `sqrt(xvel^2 + yvel^2)`.\n\nii.\n```python\n# Align and interpolate\naligned_times = frame_times - vidshift - goCue[trix]\nf_x = interp1d(aligned_times[valid], x[valid], kind='linear', ...)\nxpos[:, trix] = f_x(time_axis)\n# Velocity\nxv = np.gradient(xpos[:, trix])\n# Tongue-specific: NaN -> 0\nxv[np.isnan(xv)] = 0\nyv[np.isnan(yv)] = 0\n# Magnitude\nvel_mag = np.sqrt(xvel**2 + yvel**2)\n```\n\niii. Matches the reference code in `findPosition.m` (interpolation) and `findVelocity.m` (gradient, NaN->0 for tongue). The AI noted that tongue features are NOT smoothed before interpolation and NaN velocity is set to 0 (tongue not visible).\n\n## 7-c. How is `output` *Tongue velocity* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Per-session 50th percentile threshold computed from all valid trial data. Values >= threshold are coded as 1 (high), values < threshold as 0 (low). NaN values are set to 0.\n\nii.\n```python\ntongue_valid = tongue_vel[:, trial_indices]\ntongue_thresh = np.nanpercentile(tongue_valid[~np.isnan(tongue_valid)], 50)\n# ...\ntv_disc = np.zeros(len(time_axis), dtype=np.int64)\ntv_disc[tv >= tongue_thresh] = 1\ntv_disc[np.isnan(tv)] = 0\n```\n\niii. Follows instruction: \"discretized into two bins with per-session threshold: 0: < 50th percentile, 1: >= 50th percentile\". However, the tongue velocity distribution is heavily skewed because NaN tongue values (tongue not visible) are set to 0 velocity, making the 50th percentile very small or zero. This causes extreme class imbalance (5.7% low, 94.3% high across the dataset).\n\n## 7-d. How is `output` *Tongue velocity* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. Frame times are aligned to goCue with video offset correction, then positions are interpolated to the neural time axis using linear interpolation. Velocity is computed on the interpolated positions.\n\nii.\n```python\naligned_times = frame_times - vidshift - goCue[trix]\nf_x = interp1d(aligned_times[valid], x[valid], kind='linear', ...)\nxpos[:, trix] = f_x(time_axis)\n```\n\niii. Matches `findPosition.m`: `interp1(traj(trix).frameTimes-vidshift-obj.bp.ev.(alignEv)(trix), ts, taxis)`.\n\n## 8-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Paw velocity* derived from?\n\ni. Derived from `obj.traj{2}` (camera 1 / top camera), feature 'top_paw'. Uses x, y coordinates and frame times.\n\nii.\n```python\npaw_vel = extract_feature_velocity(session, 1, 'top_paw', time_axis, vidshift)\n```\n\niii. The AI identified from `getDefaultParams.m` that `params.traj_features{2}` includes 'top_paw' from camera 1.\n\n## 8-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Paw velocity*?\n\ni. Same pipeline as tongue but with non-tongue-specific handling: (1) Extract x, y from DLC, (2) Align and interpolate to neural time axis, (3) Fill NaN positions with nearest valid value, (4) Compute velocity via `np.gradient()`, (5) Subtract baseline derivative (median of diff), (6) Fill NaN velocity with nearest, (7) Compute magnitude.\n\nii.\n```python\n# Non-tongue: fill missing positions\nif not is_tongue:\n    xpos[:, trix] = _fill_nearest(xpos[:, trix])\n    ypos[:, trix] = _fill_nearest(ypos[:, trix])\n# Velocity with baseline subtraction\nif not is_tongue:\n    base_x = np.nanmedian(np.diff(xpos[:, trix]))\n    base_y = np.nanmedian(np.diff(ypos[:, trix]))\n    xv = xv - base_x\n    yv = yv - base_y\n    xv = _fill_nearest(xv)\n    yv = _fill_nearest(yv)\n```\n\niii. Matches `findVelocity.m`: baseline subtraction (`basederiv = median(diff(tsinterp),'omitnan')`) and `fillmissing(,'nearest')` for non-tongue features.\n\n## 8-c. How is `output` *Paw velocity* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Same as tongue: per-session 50th percentile. Values >= threshold -> 1, < threshold -> 0. NaN -> 0.\n\nii.\n```python\npaw_valid = paw_vel[:, trial_indices]\npaw_thresh = np.nanpercentile(paw_valid[~np.isnan(paw_valid)], 50)\npv_disc = np.zeros(len(time_axis), dtype=np.int64)\npv_disc[pv >= paw_thresh] = 1\npv_disc[np.isnan(pv)] = 0\n```\n\niii. Follows instructions. Paw velocity is more balanced (~50/50) since paw tracking is more consistently available.\n\n## 8-d. How is `output` *Paw velocity* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. Same as tongue: frame times aligned to goCue with video offset, then interpolated to neural time axis.\n\nii.\n```python\naligned_times = frame_times - vidshift - goCue[trix]\nf_x = interp1d(aligned_times[valid], x[valid], ...)\nxpos[:, trix] = f_x(time_axis)\n```\n\niii. Same alignment approach as tongue velocity. Matches `findPosition.m`.\n\n## 9-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Motion energy* derived from?\n\ni. Loaded from separate `motionEnergy_ANIMAL_DATE.mat` files. The variable `me.data` contains per-trial motion energy time series (at 400 Hz video frame rate). `me.moveThresh` stores a movement threshold (not used for discretization here).\n\nii.\n```python\ndef load_motion_energy(me_filepath):\n    data = sio.loadmat(me_filepath, squeeze_me=False)\n    me_raw = data['me']\n    # Handle struct format: me.data, me.moveThresh\n    # Handle double-nested: me.data.data\n    ...\n    return {'data': me_trials, 'moveThresh': me_thresh}\n```\n\niii. From `loadMotionEnergy.m`: loads `motionEnergy_*.mat` files containing per-trial motion energy data.\n\n## 9-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Motion energy*?\n\ni. Processing: (1) Load per-trial motion energy vectors, (2) Get frame times from camera 0 trajectory data, (3) Align to goCue using video offset: `frameTimes - vidshift - goCue[trial]`, (4) Interpolate to neural time axis, (5) Fill NaN with nearest valid value.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef align_motion_energy(me_data, session, time_axis, vidshift):\n    for trix in range(min(Ntrials, n_me_trials)):\n        # Get frame times from camera 0\n        frame_times = trial_traj['frameTimes']\n        aligned_times = frame_times - vidshift - goCue[trix]\n        f_me = interp1d(aligned_times[:n], trial_me[:n], kind='linear', ...)\n        me_aligned[:, trix] = f_me(time_axis)\n    # Fill NaN\n    for trix in range(Ntrials):\n        me_aligned[:, trix] = _fill_nearest(me_aligned[:, trix])\n```\n\niii. Matches `loadMotionEnergy.m`.\n\n## 10. How are minor mistakes in the data, e.g. missing data, handled?\n\ni. Multiple data quality issues are handled:\n- **Missing trajectory data**: Trials with `NdroppedFrames = NaN` are skipped (trajectory set to NaN).\n- **Missing frame times**: Fallback to synthetic frame times `np.arange(1, n_frames+1) / 400`.\n- **Missing motion energy files**: Session gets all-NaN motion energy.\n- **NaN tongue velocity**: Set to 0 (tongue not visible).\n- **NaN paw/non-tongue velocity**: Filled with nearest valid value.\n- **NaN positions (non-tongue)**: Filled with nearest valid value.\n- **Failed HDF5 references**: Empty unit lists created for invalid probes.\n- **All-zero neural trials**: Left in data (30 trials in sessions 35 and 42 have all-zero neural data from late trials where recording ended).\n\nii.\n```python\n# Missing trajectory\nif trial['ts'] is None:\n    continue\nif np.isnan(trial['NdroppedFrames']):\n    continue\n\n# Missing frame times fallback\nif trial_traj is None or trial_traj['frameTimes'] is None:\n    frame_times = np.arange(1, n_frames + 1) / VIDEO_FS\n\n# Invalid probes\nif not isinstance(clu_group, h5py.Group) or 'tm' not in clu_group:\n    clu_list.append([])\n```\n\niii. The AI documented edge cases in CONVERSION_NOTES.md Step 10. Missing data handling follows the reference code patterns (e.g., `fillmissing('nearest')` in MATLAB becomes `_fill_nearest()` in Python).\n\n## 11-a. What are the most time-consuming steps of the code?\n\ni. Based on the conversion output timing:\n- **Data loading** (~2-5s per session): Loading large HDF5/.mat files.\n- **Spike processing** (~1-5s per session): Iterating over all units and trials to bin and smooth spikes.\n- **Trajectory extraction** (~1-3s per session per feature): Interpolating DLC tracking data for tongue and paw.\n- Total full conversion: ~303s (5.1 min) for 47 sessions.\n\nii.\n```python\n# Timing is printed per step:\nprint(f'    Loaded in {t_load:.1f}s')\nprint(f'    Spike processing: {t_spk:.1f}s')\nprint(f'    Tongue velocity: {t_tongue:.1f}s')\nprint(f'    Paw velocity: {t_paw:.1f}s')\nprint(f'    Motion energy: {t_me:.1f}s')\n```\n\niii. The main inefficiency is the double loop in `process_spikes()` iterating over units and trials individually. This could be vectorized by processing all spikes for a unit at once using vectorized histogram operations. The `_fill_nearest` function also has a loop that could be vectorized using `np.interp` or pandas `fillna`.\n\nii.\n```python\n# Inner loop over trials in process_spikes:\nfor i, u_idx in enumerate(valid_units):\n    for j in range(Ntrials):\n        spk_mask = trial_nums == trial_num\n        counts, _ = np.histogram(aligned_times, bins=edges)\n        fr_smooth = smooth_signal(fr, SMOOTH_N)\n        firing_rates[i, :, j] = fr_smooth\n\n# _fill_nearest loop:\nfor i in range(len(arr)):\n    if mask[i]:\n        dists = np.abs(valid - i)\n        arr[i] = arr[valid[np.argmin(dists)]]\n```\n\niii. These loops are the primary bottleneck but the total runtime (~5 min) is acceptable.\n\n## 11-c. What processing does the code repeat multiple times?\n\ni. The video offset (`compute_video_offset`) is computed once per session, which is correct. However, the `extract_feature_velocity` function is called separately for tongue and paw, and each call independently loops through all trials to do interpolation - the frame time alignment computation is repeated. The smoothing kernel is recomputed for every unit/trial in `smooth_signal` via `causal_gaussian_kernel`.\n\nii.\n```python\n# Smoothing kernel recomputed every call:\ndef smooth_signal(x, N):\n    kern = causal_gaussian_kernel(N)  # recomputed each time\n    return np.convolve(x, kern, mode='same')\n```\n\niii. The kernel could be computed once and reused.\n\n## 11-d. What unnecessary processing does the code do that is discarded in downstream analyses?\n\ni. The code processes trajectory data for ALL trials before filtering to valid trials. Firing rates are computed for ALL `Ntrials` before selecting only `valid_trials`. This means spike binning/smoothing is done for early, stim, and no trials that are later discarded. Additionally, per-trial outputs for per-trial variables (lick direction, context, outcome) are broadcast to `(n_timepoints,)` arrays, using more memory than necessary for constant values.\n\nii.\n```python\n# Compute firing rates for ALL trials, then filter:\nfiring_rates = np.zeros((len(valid_units), n_time, Ntrials), dtype=np.float32)\n# ...later:\nfor trial_idx in trial_indices:  # only use valid trials\n    neural = firing_rates[:, :, trial_idx]\n```\n\niii. Processing all trials before filtering is simpler but wasteful.
